# Purchases (new to brand) (Amazon retail conversions)

The Purchases (new to brand) metric family contains 63 fields, listed below:

## Purchases (new to brand)

- **Reporting Field ID**: `metric.newToBrandPurchases`
- **Data Type**: `LONG`
- **Program eligibility**: [Sponsored Brands](guides/sponsored-brands/overview), [Sponsored Display](guides/sponsored-display/overview), [Sponsored TV](guides/sponsored-tv/overview), [Amazon DSP](guides/dsp/developer-guide)
- **Description**: The number of purchases by shoppers who had not purchased a product from the brand in the last 12 months.
- **Required fields**: N/A
- **Complementary fields**: N/A
- **Compatible dimensions**: [Ad](guides/reporting/ads-v1/dimensions/level-of-detail/ad), [Ad group](guides/reporting/ads-v1/dimensions/level-of-detail/ad-group), ## Cost per purchase (new to brand)

- **Reporting Field ID**: `metric.costPerNewToBrandPurchase`
- **Data Type**: `DECIMAL`
- **Program eligibility**: [Sponsored Brands](guides/sponsored-brands/overview), [Sponsored Display](guides/sponsored-display/overview), [Sponsored TV](guides/sponsored-tv/overview), [Amazon DSP](guides/dsp/developer-guide)
- **Description**: The average cost to acquire a purchase from a shopper who had  not purchased a product from the brand in the last 12 months. Cost per purchase (new to brand) = total cost / purchases (new to brand))
